# 輿圖舊序

子幼讀書知九州山川及觀史司馬氏周遊天下慨然慕焉後登會稽泛洞庭縱遊荊襄流覽
淮泗歷韓魏齊魯之郊結轍燕趙而京都實在焉繇是奉天子命祠嵩高南至于桐柏又南至
于祝融至于海往往訊遺黎尋故迹考郡邑之因革思構山川之名實驗諸淦陽安陸石刻禹迹
圖建安混一六合郡邑圖乃知前人所作殊為乖謬思殫其心力正之閱魏郡道元註水經
通典元和郡縣志宋元豐九域志今秘府大一統志黎攷古今量校遠近既得其詭而未敢自
是也中朝士夫使于四方遐邇攸同冠蓋相望則每囑以質諸藩府博采羣言隨地爲圖乃合
而爲一自至大辛亥迄延祐庚申而叨始成其間河山繡錯城連徑屬旁通正出布置曲折靡
不精到若夫漲海之東南沙漠之西北諸蕃異域錐朝貢時至而遼絕罕稽信言之者既不能詳
詳者又未可信故於斯類姑用闕如嗟乎子自總角至於四方及今二毛討論殆遍茲圖蓋其
平生之志而十年之力也後之覽者庶知其非苟云是歲日南至臨川朱思本初父自叙

---

# 廣輿圖序

子少苦羸病觀素難諸書頗慕養生嘗嘿坐一室篩縮食飲不旬日而嬴更甚始謀之醫醫曰
病在結聚非藥餌鍼灸緩不及事也為取明堂圖指任督奇經及諸手足陰陽所屬按穴而灸
繼之湯液愽輔以梁肉數月乃愈因謝醫曰標本緩急之論豈可誣哉其後往來京師從友人聞
天下緩急大勢始知考次古今名人經略之迹獨恨於山川險夷郡邑聯絡有不得盡聞者則
既無以即其形實以究其當時趨避取舍之所在况得校論其失得哉嘗偏觀天下圖籍雜怪
詳盡其疎密失準遠近錯誤百篇而一莫之能易也訪求三年間偶得元人朱思本圖其圖有計
里畫方之法而形實自是可據從而分合東西相侔不中背舛於是悉所見開增其未備因廣
其圖至于數十其諸公輩統馭不能盡載名具副本山中無外力備事積以十餘寒暑而後成夫
一人之身雖經絡升降腑臟表裏理關竅隱注殊各異稱不可揣契然庸醫投以艾石無不中其
會而動其機者豈非圖之尺寸有據哉朱之畫方似已朱為撫之臨川人博學多聞踪跡徧海
內自叙此圖乃其十年之力苟非足目所及未敢遽書可謂用心之勤至其所為畫方之法則
巧思者不逮也然考郡史不載姓名其圖亦不多見豈所謂本之則無矣乎嗚呼又安知吾之
諸圖之不為長物也於是而後悔之盖身既病誤用其心失緩急矣序而藏之用以自咎

按朱之圖長廣七尺不便卷舒今據畫方蓋易編簡仰惟

## 大明麗天

聲教無外遠軼古今可以觀德作輿地總圖一內畿外邦域民建守小大相承動無
遺法作兩直隸十三布政司圖十六王公設險安不忘危夷夏大防嚴在疆圉作九邊圖一
一一山谷藏疾時作弗靖虺兕竄伏功在刊滌作洮河松潘虔鎮麻陽諸圖五旴治宣
房載歌大海亘河新河通勢并黃河圖三不虞作桑乾河卒二瘕嶺時王孔艱圖不倣治極也
圖三四海會同濱朔翰海昂勩淮徐踪河用備水陸統計運道卒歲四夷來王且報華國不佞入
作朝鮮溯漢安南西域圖四終焉凡分圖附麗統馭更互難以旁綴者各爲副圖六十之山
川城邑名狀交錯書不盡言易以省文二十有四正誤補遺是在觀者自省

A traditional Chinese map titled "與地總圖" (General Map of the Earth), depicted with a rectangular coordinate grid. The map encompasses the Ming/Qing dynasty territory of China and neighboring regions. Key features include:
- The Great Wall stretching across the northern frontier.
- Major rivers, including the Yellow River (黃河) and the Yangtze River (江), shown with winding paths.
- Coastal and neighboring areas such as Korea (朝鮮) to the east, Liaodong (遼東), and various southern regions like Annam (安南).
- Interior labels for various administrative divisions, primarily Prefectures (府) and Departments (州).
- Stylized mountain ranges, with specific focus on the Five Sacred Peaks (五嶽).
- Marginal regions labeled like Hami (哈密) to the northwest and Tibet (西藏) to the west.
</image-desc>

# 輿地總圖二

大明輿地東起朝鮮西至嘉峪南濱海北連沙漠道路紆縈各萬餘里
南北直隸府二十二屬州三十四縣二百一十承宣布政使司十三屬府一百一十八羈縻一十九州一百五十三羈縻四十七縣九百二十八羈縻六總為府一百五十九州二百三十四縣一千一百一十四實計里六萬九千五百五十六戶九百三十五萬二千零一十五口五千八百五十五萬八百零一

# 北直隸輿圖

每方百里府從□州從◇縣從○衛從■後仿此

<image-desc>
A traditional Chinese woodblock-printed map of Northern Zhili province, depicting administrative divisions, topography, and hydrography. The map is overlaid with a regular square grid, where each square represents 100 square li. 

The coastline of the Bohai Sea is shown on the right (East), with wavy lines indicating water. Major mountain ranges are depicted with stylized peaks. Numerous rivers (such as the Luan River, Sanggan River, and Hutuo River) are drawn as meandering lines flowing across the landscape. 

The map identifies major administrative centers using specific symbols:
- Squares (□) for Prefectures (Fu) such as 順天府 (Shuntian), 保定府 (Baoding), 河間府 (Hejian), 真定府 (Zhending), 順德府 (Shunde), 廣平府 (Guangping), 大名府 (Daming), and 永平府 (Yongping).
- Diamonds (◇) for Departments (Zhou).
- Circles (○) for Counties (Xian).
- Solid squares (■) for Guards (Wei) or Garrisons.

To the north (top) and west (left) are areas outside the primary provincial administration, including locations like 宣府 (Xuanfu), 建州 (Jianzhou), and various border passes along the Great Wall region. The bottom left corner contains a boxed label "第四" indicating its sequence in a larger atlas.
</image-desc>

- 通州
- 天津衛

- 真定府
- 順德府
- 廣平府
- 大名府
- 滹沱河
- 衛河

**邊界與外部區域 (Border/Outer regions):**
- 遼東 (top right edge)
- 漠北 (top center)
- 山西界 (left edge)
- 山東界 (bottom right)
- 河南界 (bottom center)

<image-desc>
This page features a highly structured administrative summary common in late imperial Chinese gazetteers. The right side contains descriptive text detailing census figures (households, population), tax quotas (grain, silk, cotton, salt), and military troop levels (garrisons, cavalry, and guards). The left two-thirds of the page consists of a large, complex grid listing administrative units including Prefectures (府), Departments (州), Counties (縣), Garrisons (衛), and Strategic Posts (所/關城). Small annotations within the grid provide distances (measured in li) and subordinate relationships.
</image-desc>

<image-desc>
This is a historical Chinese administrative diagram/map titled "Northern Zhili Map 3" (北直隸輿圖三), likely from the late Ming or early Qing dynasty. The layout uses a structured grid to represent the administrative hierarchy of Northern Zhili province. Large characters identify key Departments (州), while vertical lists beneath them specify subordinate counties. Each entry is accompanied by extensive technical annotations in smaller script detailing land tax quotas (such as "autumn grain" or 秋粮), silver obligations (銀), and historical administrative changes.

The map includes several major prefectures and departments:
- Right side: Jizhou (冀州), Jinzhou (晉州), Zhaozhou (趙州), Shenzhou (深州), Qizhou (祁州), and Dingzhou (定州).
- Middle: Anzhou (安州), Yizhou (易州), and sections for Baoding (保定) and Hejian (河間).
- Left side: Mentions of administrative offices like the Governor (巡撫) and Governor General (總督), as well as Tianjin (天津).

A dense grid at the bottom lists the individual counties and administrative centers included in this section of the atlas.
</image-desc>

<header>山東輿圖 每方一百里</header>
<sig>第九十一</sig>

<image-desc>
這是一幅山東省的木刻版畫輿圖，採用計里畫方的繪製方式，圖中佈滿正方形網格，右上角標註「每方一百里」。地圖描繪了山東全境的海岸線、島嶼、河流及山脈。

地圖東部為山東半島，向東北延伸至海中，北隔渤海與「遼東」相望。圖中詳細標註了各級行政單位，包括濟南府、青州府、登州府、萊州府、兗州府和東昌府等六府，以及下轄的州、縣、衛、所。

地理特徵方面，地圖左側可見大運河流經，中部繪有著名的泰山（標註為「泰山」）。海岸線周邊繪有水波紋，並標記了多個島嶼如「沙門島」。河流系統複雜，展現了黃河（當時經山東入海的流路）及其支流。整幅圖文字密集，均為繁體漢字，記錄了明清時期的地理佈局。
</image-desc>

<header>山東輿圖三 東充道</header>
<meta>Top left margin label: 海右</meta>

<image-desc>
This historical document is an administrative chart or organizational map of the "Dongchong" circuit within Shandong province during the Qing dynasty. The layout uses vertical grid lines to organize administrative jurisdictions from right to left. At the top are titles for high-ranking regional officials and oversight offices (such as "分守兖東道" and "兵備副使"). Below these are larger boxed headings for Prefectures (府) and Departments (州), including Linqing, Gaotang, Yanzhou, Dongping, Caozhou, Jining, and Yizhou. Each section provides historical and geographic context for the area, followed by a list of subordinate counties (縣). These listings include the distance in "li" (里) from the administrative seat and a rating of the location's administrative importance and difficulty using the Qing "four-character" system: "Chong" (沖 - hub), "Fan" (繁 - busy), "Pi" (疲 - weary), and "Nan" (難 - difficult).
</image-desc>

A historical woodblock-printed map of Shanxi (山西) province and surrounding regions. The map is oriented with north at the top. A prominent grid covers the entire map area, with a note in the title box stating that each square represents 100 li (每方一百里). 

The map depicts administrative divisions (prefectures, subprefectures, and counties), major cities, significant mountain ranges (represented by stylized peaks), and river systems. The Yellow River (黄河) is clearly visible, forming the western and southern borders of the province. Significant landmarks such as the Great Wall and various mountain passes (indicated by gate-like icons) are included. 

To the west and north of Shanxi, labels include "河套" (Ordos Loop), "綏德" (Suide), and various military outposts. To the east lie "真定" (Zhending) and "井陘" (Jingxing). The southern border is defined by the Yellow River and labels for regions like "孟" (Meng) and "輝" (Hui). Inside Shanxi, major centers such as "大同" (Datong), "大原" (Taiyuan, labeled as 大原府), and "潞" (Lu) are prominent.
</image-desc>

Below these headers, subordinate districts (counties/Xiàn) and relay stations are listed alongside detailed historical notes, administrative classifications (such as "冲煩" indicating high traffic and administrative complexity), and distances measured in "li" (里). <header>陝西輿圖 一每方一百里</header>

<image-desc>
A traditional Chinese woodblock-printed map of Shaanxi Province, utilizing a grid system (known as "Jingli") where each square represents 100 "li" (approximately 50 kilometers). The map's orientation features North at the top. 

Key geographic features include:
1. **Waterways:** The Yellow River (黄河) defines much of the northern and eastern boundaries. The Wei River (渭水) flows horizontally across the center through the Guanzhong Plain. The Han River (漢水) flows through the southern mountainous region.
2. **Topography:** Stylized mountain ranges are scattered throughout, with notable peaks like Mount Hua (華山) near the eastern pass and the Qinling Mountains (終南山) south of the provincial capital.
3. **Man-made structures:** The Great Wall (長城) is depicted with crenelated lines along the northern frontier near Yulin and Ningxia.
4. **Administrative Centers:** Major cities (Prefectures or "Fu") are marked with square icons, often enclosed in double borders. Smaller towns (Counties or "Xian" and Departments or "Zhou") are marked with smaller icons or plain text. 

The map includes numerous labels for passes (關), garrisons (衛), and regional borders.
</image-desc>

# 陝西輿圖二

<image-desc>
A traditional Chinese cartographic map of the northwestern frontier (modern Gansu, Ningxia, and eastern Qinghai regions), part of a series labeled "Shaanxi Map 2." The map is presented on a rectangular coordinate grid, a style characteristic of Ming dynasty atlases like the "Guang Yu Tu." 

Topographical features are depicted through stylized illustrations: 
- Mountain ranges are shown as clusters of conical peaks.
- Major rivers, including the Yellow River (黃河) and the Black River (黑河/Ejin River), are drawn with flowing lines.
- The Great Wall is represented as a dashed line punctuated with rectangular fortresses and gates.
- Administrative centers (Fu, Zhou, Xian) are marked with square-walled icons containing their names, while smaller outposts and forts are marked with dots or small circles.

Key locations depicted include the Silk Road cities of Shazhou (Dunhuang), Suzhou (Jiuquan), Ganzhou (Zhangye), Liangzhou (Wuwei), and Lanzhou, as well as the border passes of Yangguan and Jiayuguan.
</image-desc>

<header>河南輿圖 每方一百里</header>

<image-desc>
這是一幅繪製在方格網上的河南省傳統行政地圖，地圖標題位於右側縱向長方框內，書有「河南輿圖 每方一百里」。地圖展示了河南省及周邊地區的山川、河流、府州縣城。

黃河橫貫地圖中北部。圖中標註了多個重要行政區域：
- 北部：彰德府、衛輝府、懷慶府。
- 中部：河南府（洛陽）、開封府、歸德府、許州、汝州、陳州、陝州。
- 南部：汝寧府、南陽府。

圖中精細地描繪了山脈（如太行山、嵩山、伏牛山等，以山形符號表示）和河流系。城鎮以方框或圓圈標記。地圖右上角有一行文字標註河流走向：「衛河至館陶合泲」。地圖左下角有「第十五」字樣的頁碼標記。
</image-desc>

衛河至館陶合泲
</margin>

<header>浙江輿圖二</header>

浙江等處承宣布政使司治杭州左布政使二右布政使二左右參政五<gloss>管糧一杭州嘉湖</gloss>
議二<gloss>温處一金衢嚴一</gloss>領府十一州一縣七十五總為里一萬八百九十九戶一百二十四萬二千一百三十五口四百五十二萬五千四百七十一夏秋二稅共米麥一百五十一萬二百九十九石絲綿共二百七十萬四千二百七十兩絹三千五百七十四疋鈔五萬一千二百九十三錠馬草八十七萬四千四百九十一包
浙江都指揮使司<gloss>隸五軍都督府</gloss>都指揮三內掌印一僉書二領衛一十六<gloss>舊一百九十一</gloss>守禦千戶所三十五本都司所屬馬步官軍三萬九千九百餘員名
提刑按察司按察使一副使七<gloss>舊三巡海一提學一應</gloss>僉事七<gloss>水利一疏曠一溫處一分道五</gloss>
浙江都轉運鹽使司一<gloss>管轄七場水利一</gloss>
歲辦鹽四十四萬四千七百六十九引
市舶提舉司一<gloss>在寧波</gloss>

An administrative schematic of Zhejiang province from a historical atlas, organized into vertical columns. The page is structured by Circuits (道), Prefectures (府), and Counties (縣). From left to right, it displays the administrative divisions for Wen-Chu Circuit, Jin-Qu Circuit, and Shao Circuit. <header>江西輿圖一方百里</header>
<page-num>第二</page-num>

<image-desc>
這是一幅清代木刻版的江西省輿圖，採用傳統的「計里畫方」法繪製。地圖上覆蓋著約 13 乘 15 的方格網，右上角的標題框註明「一方百里」，即每一方格代表一百里。地圖詳細標註了省城、各府、州、縣的行政位置。河流（如貫穿全境的贛江）以蜿蜒的線條表示，山脈則以密集的山峰符號描繪，尤其在省界邊緣處如梅嶺、武夷山、仙霞嶺等。地圖四周邊界外還標註了相鄰省份（如楚、閩、粵、吳）的接壤地名。
</image-desc>

### 地圖標註文字（按區域劃分）

**標題：**
江西輿圖一方百里

**北境與周邊地名：**
宿松、黃梅、口九江、瑞昌、德安、星子、建昌、都昌、湖口、彭澤、東流、建德、祁門、婺源、歙縣、休寧、黟縣、績溪。

**東境與周邊地名：**
開化、懷玉山、常山、江山、仙霞嶺、崇安、光澤、邵武、汀州。

**西境與周邊地名：**
通城、崇陽、蒲圻、嘉魚、醴陵、攸縣、茶陵、桂東、桂陽、宜章、樂昌、乳源。

**南境與周邊地名：**
始興、翁源、英德、龍門、花縣、長寧、尋鄔、定南、龍南、信豐、大庾。

**省內各府、州、縣標註：**
*   **南昌府（省城）**：新建、南昌、豐城、進賢、奉新、安義、靖安、武寧、寧縣。
*   **饒州府**：鄱陽、餘干、樂平、德興、安仁、萬年。
*   **廣信府**：上饒、玉山、弋陽、貴溪、鉛山、永豐。
*   **建昌府**：南城、新城、南豐、廣昌、瀘溪。
*   **撫州府**：臨川、崇仁、金溪、宜黃、樂安。
*   **臨江府**：清江、新淦、新喻、峽江。
*   **瑞州府**：高安、上高、新昌。
*   **袁州府**：宜春、分宜、萍鄉、萬載。
*   **吉安府**：廬陵、泰和、吉水、永豐、安福、龍泉、萬安、永寧。
*   **贛州府**：贛縣、雩都、信豐、興國、會昌、安遠、長寧、龍南、定南、尋鄔。
*   **寧都州**：石城、瑞金。
*   **南安府**：大庾、南康、上猶、崇義。

**主要山川地理：**
廬山、鄱陽湖、贛水、梅嶺、大庾嶺、武夷山、仙霞嶺、三爪崙。

->每方百里 宣慰司 从 ■ 宣撫司 从 ◩ 安撫司 从 ○ 長官司 从 △ 後似此<-

<image-desc>
This is a historical woodblock-printed map of Huguang Province (present-day Hubei and Hunan) from the Ming or early Qing dynasty. The map is overlaid with a uniform square grid system; the legend on the right edge notes that each square represents 100 li (每方百里). 

**Cartographic Details:**
- **Waterways:** The Yangtze River (長江) flows through the center, meeting the Han River (漢水) at Wuchang and Hanyang. Dongting Lake (洞庭湖) is a central feature, receiving the four major rivers of Hunan: the Xiang (湘), Zi (資), Yuan (沅), and Li (澧).
- **Topography:** Mountains are represented by small, stylized triangular peaks, with higher density in the western borderlands (modern Enshi and western Hunan) and the southern ranges.
- **Administrative Labels:** Major prefectures (府) and departments (州) are clearly labeled, including Wuchang (武昌), Hanyang (漢陽), Jingzhou (荊州), Xiangyang (襄陽), Chengtian (承天), De'an (德安), Huangzhou (黃州), Yuezhou (岳州), Changsha (長沙), Changde (常德), Chenzhou (辰州), Yuanzhou (沅州), Baoqing (寶慶), Hengzhou (衡州), and Yongzhou (永州).
- **Tribal Jurisdictions:** The western regions include numerous semi-autonomous administrative units marked with symbols defined in the legend, such as the Xuanwei Si (宣慰司) of Yongshun (永順) and Baojing (保靖).
- **Surrounding Regions:** Marginal text outside the grid marks neighboring provinces: Henan (河南) to the north; Jiangxi (江西) to the east; Guangdong (廣東) and Guangxi (廣西) to the south; and Sichuan (四川) and Guizhou (貴州) to the west.
</image-desc>

# 四川輿圖

->一每方百里摺<-
->一計司亦从而<-

<image-desc>
A traditional Chinese woodblock-printed map of Sichuan Province, rendered with a rectangular grid layout. The title "四川輿圖" (Sichuan Map) is enclosed in a vertical box at the top right. Below the title, a legend notes that each square of the grid represents one hundred square li ("每方百里").

The map detail includes:
- **Administrative Divisions:** Major centers such as Chengdu (成都府), Chongqing (重慶府), Baoning (保寧府), Shunqing (順慶府), and Kuizhou (夔州府) are prominently labeled. Prefectures (府), departments (州), and counties (縣) are distributed throughout the basin and mountain regions.
- **Topography:** Stylized mountain ranges and a complex hydrological network depicting the Yangtze River (Jinsha Jiang) and its tributaries (Min Jiang, Tuo Jiang, Jialing Jiang) are illustrated.
- **Borders:** The map indicates boundaries with neighboring regions: Shaanxi (陕西界) to the north, Huguang (湖廣界) to the east, Guizhou (貴州界) and Yunnan (雲南界) to the south, and the western tribal frontier (西番界) to the west.
- **Annotations:** Numerous small labels designate river crossings, passes, local settlements, and strategic points. Grid lines are used for geographic scale and relative positioning.
</image-desc>

# 川北道

## 保寧
府治 **閬中** 蜀中名郡 封巴子國 晉置梁州 唐為保寧軍 皇朝初為省治 領縣七 賦入秋米二萬五千零

<margin>威州 雖附保寧 實裁併 距府下六百里</margin>

**閬中**
附郭 閬山在內 巴西 距府十里

**蒼溪**
府北 四十里 舊屬閬中 隋置縣

**南部**
距府下百里 晉置縣 隋屬保寧

**昭化**
漢晉壽地 宋名昭化 距府下六十里

**廣元**
即秦葭萌地 漢為梓潼地 隋名廣元 距府下百二十里

**通江**
隋置縣 皇朝併 距府下百六十里

**巴州**
古巴子國地 梁置巴州 賦入秋米五千四百四 領縣二

A traditional Chinese administrative map of the Sichuan region, titled "Sichuan Map 5" (四川輿圖五). The map is constructed using a "jili huafang" grid system, where each square represents a consistent spatial measurement. Within the grid, various locations are identified with their names and, in some cases, detailed notes on their administrative jurisdiction (e.g., "隷四川" - belongs to Sichuan; "隷貴州" - belongs to Guizhou). Key regions include Bozhou (播州), Lizhou (黎州), and Jianchang (建昌). The map also features notes on geographical features like the Dadu River (大渡河) and regional military commissions (都司). Marginal labels outside the grid provide directional markers and references to neighboring maps.
</image-desc>

# 福建輿圖二

福建等處承宣布政使司治福州左、右使二，左右参政三，左右参議三。
<gloss>糧儲</gloss>領府八州一縣五十有三，總為里三千七百九十七里有零，戶五十
萬九千二百口二百八萬二千六百七十七。夏秋二税共米麥八十八萬
三千一百一十五石，絲綿一百九十四兩，絹六百疋，苧六十五斤，鈔一萬
七百七十九錠。

福建都轉運鹽使司一<gloss>在福</gloss>領鹽課司七，歳辨塩價八千八百七十八兩。
福建都指揮使司<gloss>縁置建都暫附</gloss>都指揮三内掌印一，僉書二，領衞一十一<gloss>一属所一</gloss>。
十七建寧行都指揮使司都指揮三，領衛五<gloss>属所一</gloss>守禦千戶所四，所屬馬
歩官軍共四萬八千二百餘真名。

提刑按察司按察使一，副使四<gloss>巡海一，清軍一，分守一，分巡一</gloss>，僉事五<gloss>兵備二，分巡一，</gloss>
欽差提督浙福都御史一<gloss>駐福</gloss>
巡按御史一，或清軍一<gloss>俱煩</gloss>

### 每方一百里

<image-desc>
A traditional Chinese woodblock-printed map of Guizhou Province (貴州), overlaid with a rectangular grid. The legend at the top right indicates that each square of the grid represents 100 li (每方一百里). The map shows a highly mountainous terrain, with numerous peaks indicated by small triangular icons. Major river systems, including the Wujiang (烏江) in the center and northern parts, are depicted with winding lines. Administrative divisions such as prefectures (府), departments (州), and counties (縣) are labeled throughout. The map also indicates neighboring regions such as Sichuan (四川) to the north, Huguang (湖廣) to the east, and Guangxi (慶遠, 柳州) to the south. In the bottom left corner, there is a small boxed label containing the characters "第五十九" (Number 59).
</image-desc>

# 輿圖

<vocab>輿圖, yútú, atlas, map</vocab>

--- Page 62 ---
<lang>zh-Hant</lang>
<page-type>map</page-type>
<meta>Woodblock print map with a 500-li grid system. Includes the Great Wall and the Nine Frontiers of the Ming Dynasty.</meta>

<image-desc>
A traditional Chinese woodblock-printed map titled "九邊總圖" (General Map of the Nine Frontiers). The map uses a "Jiuzhou" grid style, where each square represents "五百里" (500 li), as noted in the title box.

The map depicts the Ming Dynasty's territory. Along the top (North), the Great Wall is prominently featured with labels for the nine strategic frontier commands (Liaodong, Jizhou, Xuanfu, Datong, Shanxi, Yansui, Ningxia, Guyuan, and Gansu). To the top right, the Korean Peninsula (朝鮮) and the Yalu River (鴨綠江) are visible.

To the right (East), the coastline is rendered with stylized wave patterns. Major coastal cities and provinces like Nanjing (南京), Hangzhou (杭州), and Fujian (福建) are marked. 

The interior displays the administrative divisions of the Ming Empire, including provincial capitals, prefectures (府), and major rivers like the Yellow River (黃河) and Yangtze River (長江). Mountains are illustrated as clusters of small, jagged peaks.

In the bottom left (Southwest) are the mountainous regions of Yunnan (雲南) and Guizhou (貴州). The bottom center shows Guangdong (廣東) and the island of Hainan (瓊州). 
</image-desc>

### 北部疆界與九邊 (Northern Frontier)

A historical woodblock-printed map of the Liaodong Peninsula and its northern frontier, featuring a rectangular coordinate grid. The title "Liaodong Border Map, each square equals 100 li" is located in the upper left. 

Key features of the map include:
*   **The Border Wall:** A prominent dotted line representing a defensive wall (likely the Willow Palisade or the Liaodong portion of the Great Wall) winds across the upper and right portions of the map, punctuated by numerous gates and watchtowers marked with triangular symbols.
*   **Hydrography:** The coastline of the Bohai Gulf and Yellow Sea is depicted with stylized, swirling wave patterns. Major river systems like the Liao River (遼河), Hun River (渾河), Taizi River (太子河), and Yalu River (鴨綠江) are shown flowing through the region.
*   **Topography:** Stylized mountain ranges are distributed across the landscape, including labels like Pingshan (平山) and Jinshan (金山).
*   **Settlements:** Administrative centers and major towns are indicated by square-bordered text boxes. Notable locations include Guangning (廣寧), Shenyang (瀋陽), Liaoyang (遼陽), Kaiyuan (開元), and Haizhou (海州). Numerous smaller military outposts and villages are labeled throughout the interior and along the frontier.
*   **Peninsula:** The southern Liaodong Peninsula is clearly visible, showing settlements like Fuzhou (復州), Jinzhou (金州), and Lushunkou (旅順口) at the very tip.
</image-desc>

<image-desc>
A historical woodblock-printed map depicting the "Inner Three Passes" (內三關) border region of North China, likely from the Ming or early Qing dynasty. The map is overlaid with a coordinate grid, with a note stating that "each square equals 100 li" (每方百里). The geography is shown with traditional pictorial representations: mountain ranges are drawn as clusters of peaks, and rivers are depicted as winding lines. The map is densely populated with labels in traditional Chinese characters identifying administrative seats (marked with circles or squares), mountain passes (關), fortresses, and natural features. Significant landmarks include the Great Wall passes like Daoma Pass (倒馬關), Zijing Pass (紫荊關), and Juyong Pass (居庸關), as well as major cities like Baoding (保定), Dingzhou (定州), and Zhending (真定).
</image-desc>

# 直隸三關

欽差巡撫保定等處都御史 一駐保定
欽差巡按左都等關察院一駐北京

A hand-drawn, woodblock-printed style map of the Xuanfu (宣府) border region during the Ming Dynasty, laid out on a grid system. The map depicts the strategic defensive landscape northwest of Beijing (京師), which is shown on the right. Key features include mountain ranges (indicated by triangular symbols), the Sanggan River (桑乾河), and numerous walled cities, forts (represented by square enclosures), and mountain passes. Major locations such as Xuanfu, Huailai, Yanqing, and various military garrisons (Wanquan, Huaian, etc.) are labeled. The northern border shows the proximity to Mongolian territories with names like "Dushikou" (獨石口).

### 大同外三關邊圖 一每方百里

<image-desc>
A traditional Chinese woodblock-printed map of the Datong frontier region and the "Outer Three Passes" (偏頭關 Piantou, 寧武關 Ningwu, and 雁門關 Yanmen) in Shanxi. The map is overlaid with a grid where each square represents one hundred li (一每方百里). It depicts a rugged terrain of stylized mountain peaks and winding river systems. Military installations, passes, and administrative centers are marked with square borders and text labels. The Great Wall defense network is represented by the line of passes and forts across the lower-central portion of the map.
</image-desc>

**Map Labels and Inscriptions:**

*   **Principal Passes:** 偏頭關 (Piantou Pass), 寧武關 (Ningwu Pass), 雁門關 (Yanmen Pass).
*   **Administrative Centers (Districts and Prefectures):** 大同 (Datong), 朔州 (Shuozhou), 應州 (Yingzhou), 忻州 (Xinzhou), 代州 (Daizhou), 朔州 (Shuozhou).
*   **Towns and Counties:** 馬邑 (Mayi), 繁峙 (Fanshi), 靈丘 (Lingqiu), 廣靈 (Guangling), 渾源 (Hunyuan), 河曲 (Hequ), 保德 (Baode), 岢嵐 (Kelan), 靜樂 (Jingle), 廣昌 (Guangchang).
*   **Military Posts (Guards and Forts):** 威遠, 左衛, 右衛, 德勝堡, 懷安衛.
*   **Geographical Features:** 黃河 (Yellow River), 桑乾河 (Sanggan River), 滹沱河 (Hutuo River), 葫蘆海 (Hulu Sea), 五臺山 (Mount Wutai), 雲中山 (Mount Yunzhong), 丫角山 (Mount Yajiao).
*   **Historical/Regional Notes:** 青塚 (Qingzhong), 舊豐州 (Old Fengzhou), 即大同中屯衛.

</margin>

# 大同鎮

大同鎮屬衛八所七堡五百八十三馬步官軍舍餘士兵五萬四千一百五十四員名糧料布花屯糧也草及京運年例通共銀七十七萬五千一百八十八兩七錢五分屯糧一十二萬七千七百二十一石<gloss>每石折銀四錢</gloss>屯草一十七萬六千四百一十一束秋青草三十七萬二千一百一十束<gloss>每束折銀二分</gloss>年例銀七萬兩例塩七萬引馬四萬六千九百四十四匹<gloss>新舊額并死損補</gloss>山西起運夏秋二稅糧料二十九萬一千四百七十五石<gloss>每石折銀五錢</gloss>夏秋稅糧折布一萬八千五百五十二疋<gloss>每疋折銀六錢</gloss>棉花絨八萬斤草二百四十四萬四千八百五十束<gloss>每束折銀八分</gloss>河南起運小麥九萬六千石<gloss>每石折銀六錢</gloss>

->百七十三匹糧料一十三萬九千九百一十五石折色糧并折草銀一十四萬<-

A traditional Chinese woodblock-printed map titled "莊寧京永邊圖一 醵勘" (Frontier Map of Zhuanglang, Ningxia, Jingyuan, and Yongchang, Part 1, Surveyed). The map depicts a rugged frontier landscape with stylized mountain ranges, rivers, and sections of the Great Wall. A grid system is visible at the top of the map. Numerous place names, including military guards (衛), forts (堡), passes (口), and natural landmarks, are labeled throughout the terrain. Significant locations include 莊浪衛 (Zhuanglang Guard) near the center and 海城縣房 (Haicheng County Office) in the lower left. The map identifies various terrain features such as 苦海 (Bitter Sea), 芳溝 (Fragrant Gully), and numerous peaks. Buildings are represented by small square and rectangular icons.
</image-desc>

A traditional Chinese woodblock-printed map of the Shandan (山丹) border region in Gansu province, depicted over a square coordinate grid. The map displays a varied terrain of jagged mountain ranges and river systems, with numerous settlements, outposts, and geographic landmarks labeled in vertical Chinese text. In the center, a large mountain massif is identified as "Daqingshan" (大青山). The Heihe river system is shown flowing northward towards the top of the map. Major cities like Suzhou (肅州) and Ganzhou (張掖) are noted. The map is framed by a double-line border with a title box in the upper right and a page number box in the lower left.

# 山丹其肅邊圖五

### 甘州
衛屬堡八馬步官軍三萬三千八百九十四員名馬八千七百五十一匹糧料六萬五千七百九十七石有零民運本折糧布銀一十萬七千三百九十五兩一錢二分鹽糧二萬六千八百一十四石八斗草一百一十萬一千四百八十九束年例銀四萬兩
欽差巡撫都御史一人大僕寺卿一人分巡副使一寺丞一主簿一監收通判一判官一俱駐甘州
總兵一副總兵一俱駐甘州守備一驍騎校指揮五黑城板橋平川叶峻古城各一掌堡官二東城沙河坐營皆千管副指揮管隊官
全印屯局捕首領官全

--- Page 85 ---
<lang>zh-Hant</lang>
<page-type>map</page-type>
<header>洮河邊圖 每方一百里</header>
<meta>第十四</meta>

<image-desc>
這是一幅採木刻版畫形式製作的歷史地圖，描繪了洮河邊疆地區的地理景觀。地圖背景繪有方格網，根據右上角的標註「每方一百里」，每格代表一百里。地圖展現了複雜的地貌，包括群山、河流以及各類城鎮、關隘與軍事衛所。

主要河流包括黃河、洮河、湟水與羗水，河流以流動的線條表示。山脈則以傳統的峰巒重疊圖樣描繪，標註有如蓮花山、羊馬嶺等山名。

地圖上分佈著眾多行政與軍事據點，如蘭州、岷州、洮州、臨洮、定西、安定等城鎮，以及萬方關、天門關、陽塞等重要關隘。地圖亦標示了族群的分佈，如「番族」、「羗族」及「蒙古部落」。左側邊界標有「西軍界」，上方標有「培庸衛界」與「莊浪衛界」。

文字均為繁體中文，由右至左、由上至下排列於相關地理要素旁。
</image-desc>

<lang>zh</lang>
<page-type>map</page-type>
<page-num>第八十六</page-num>

<image-desc>
A historical woodblock-printed map of the Songpan (松潘) border region, likely from a Ming Dynasty geographical work. The map utilizes a grid system for scale, with the note "每方百里" (one square equals 100 li) in the title box. The terrain is densely depicted with stylized mountain peaks and river systems, including the Min River (岷江). Numerous labels identify administrative centers, defensive passes, and the territories of various ethnic groups. These groups are largely categorized as "Black Fan" (黑番) and "White Fan" (白番), with specific tribal names such as "Zhanbai" (占白人), "Shide" (史德人), and "Zawa" (扎瓦人) appearing in the upper regions. Major towns like Songpan, Minzhou (岷州), and Long'an (龍安) are marked with distinct icons. Several long vertical inscriptions provide historical and administrative context regarding border expansions and jurisdictional boundaries.
</image-desc>

->每方百里<-

*   **行政中心及要塞：** 松潘、龍安、岷州、洮州、疊州、文縣、茂州、威州、答州、嘉峪關、歸化關、嘉安關、小關、中關。
*   **水系：** 岷江、西河、小河。
*   **族群與部落：** 黑番、白番、生番、熟番、占白人、史德人、扎瓦人、皀那人、木瓜黑番、毛牛黑番、草坡人。

<vocab>松潘 (Songpan), 嘉靖 (Jiajing), 歸化 (Guihua), 嘉安 (Jia'an), 黑番 (Black Fan), 白番 (White Fan), 岷江 (Min River), 邊圖 (Border Map), 每方百里 (Scale: 100 li per square)</vocab>

<header>建昌圖一晦點</header>

->方格折法計縱从〇起横从〇起各一<-
->長官从△ 司从△ 驛从▲<-

<image-desc>
這是一幅標繪有方格網的中國古代區域地圖，題為「建昌圖」。地圖描繪了建昌衛（今四川西昌一帶）及其周邊地區的地理概況，包括河流、山脈、行政機構和交通驛站。

1. **方格網**：全圖覆蓋有均勻的方格，用於標示距離。
2. **地形與河流**：
    - 山脈：以傳統的山峰符號繪製，如「飛越」、「大雪山」、「小雪山」等。
    - 河流：以雙線繪製，內有波浪紋，標註有「大渡河」、「金沙江」、「瀾滄」等水系。
3. **行政與軍事地點**：
    - 中心位置標註有「建昌衛」，周邊分佈有「德昌」、「會川」、「鹽源」、「越嶲」等衛所與長官司。
    - 地圖右側標註有「馬湖府」、「烏蒙府」、「東川府」、「尋甸府」等府治。
    - 地圖左側邊緣見「北勝」及「雲南」界。
4. **符號與圖例**：
    - 右上角說明：△ 表示「長官」或「司」，▲ 表示「驛」（驛站）。圖中各處散布著這些三角形符號。
5. **地名標註**：地圖上密佈細小的地名標註，涉及土司、驛站、關隘等資訊，如「董卜」、「天全六番」、「沈邊」、「魚通」等位於地圖上方。
</image-desc>

<meta>右上方標題文字：建昌圖一晦點；下方有比例尺與符號說明。左下角有頁碼「第十七」。</meta>

# 麻陽圖
<gloss>酌芳一公分立五官司公么</gloss>

<image-desc>
A traditional Chinese topographical map of Mayang County (麻陽縣) and its surrounding administrative regions, likely from the Qing Dynasty. The map features a square grid for scale. Mountains are depicted with stylized line drawings of peaks, and rivers are drawn as winding paths. Settlement names, administrative units (such as 州, 府, 司), and village stockades (寨) are labeled in traditional Chinese characters throughout the landscape. Major regional centers like Shizhou (施州), Baojing (保靖), Yongshun (永順), and Chenzhou Prefecture (辰州府) are marked along the map's borders. The central mountainous region contains a high density of local place names. A detailed text block in the lower-right corner describes regional boundaries and ethnic settlements.
</image-desc>

匪小楠木沙灑渡接界。其南下方與山峒苗人雞嗚舖、
平子等舖、苗地相接。
麻陽子弟分由一十七里、平南、銅仁、中署、設五堡公益。十七路堡苗
皆苗。苗界六十里內由大塘、麻陽、白馬、大江、龍口、和、玉、神、大、馬、龍、池、巴、馬、神、大、五
舖、兩堡分設。兩塘、石橋、龍水、汴、劉、界、趙、龍、禹、烏、馬、玉、合、平、興、隆、
苗人皆熟苗。一十七里、平南、乾、法、河、萬、山、巴、馬、中、池、平、衡、

<header>
黃河圖一 每方一百里
第九十二
</header>

<image-desc>
此圖為木刻版畫風格的黃河下游水利圖，地圖上方繪有經緯方格，旁註「每方一百里」，用於標示比例。地圖詳細描繪了黄河、淮河及其支流、湖泊的地理分布。

畫面右側為沿海地帶，以密集的波浪紋表示海洋。陸地部分遍布河流與湖泊（如洪澤湖、石梁湖、微山湖等），并標註了大量的府、州、縣及重要地點。河流走向及堤壩位置清晰，並伴有關於河道變遷或工事的文字說明。山脈以傳統的山水畫法呈現。主要城鎮如徐州、淮安、泗州、鳳陽等均有標註。
</image-desc>

<image-desc>
這是一幅名為「黃河圖三」的歷史地圖，描繪了黃河從源頭到中游的流經區域。地圖上覆蓋著方格網格，左側文字說明「每方三百里」。地圖詳細標註了黃河流經的山脈、城市、行政區域以及里程。右下角有一大段文字，記述了漢代張騫、唐代薛元鼎、元代都實等尋訪河源的歷史過程，並提及朱思本根據相關資料繪製此圖。地圖包含崑崙山、星宿海、悶磨黎山等地理標識，以及肅州、蘭州、寧夏、延安等城市名。
</image-desc>

<insert>
黃河圖三每方三百里
</insert>

->**黃河圖**<-

<column-break/>

漢使張騫持節西域訪河源以為二水發葱嶺
趨于闐滙塩澤伏流千里至積石而再出唐薛
元鼎使吐蕃訪河源得之悶磨黎山世之論河
源者幸皆不北莫知其非也至元始命都實為
招討使佩金虎符往求之四越月而得其說如
圖所載還具以聞其弟綴撰其說并繪錄翰林學
士潘昂霄撰為記而睦川朱思本於八里吉思
家得都實師所藏梵字圖書譯之與潘昂互有小異
俱載元史黃河本東北流歷西蕃至蘭州凡二
千五百餘里始入中國又東北流逾慶境凡二
千五百餘里始轉河東又南流至蒲州凡一千
八百餘里通計屈曲九千餘里而張騫所訪乃
在其西萬里外蓋為吐蕃燕踵不經道故也
世之受蔽於外舍延求遠皆何以異此朱思本
姓名因河源記始傳其為圖與所記山水道里
不少差舛特存之以代輶軒之對或言天下之
山皆原於崑崙今觀之圖豈足與辨未能貫詣
而徇傳聞不獨於山然也

---

*   **西北部：** 肅州、嘉峪關、瓜州、沙州、敦煌、陽關、玉門關
*   **中部及河套：** 凉州、蘭州、西寧、寧夏、靈州、賀蘭山、西夏、中受降、西受降、東安寧
*   **東南部：** 延安、丹州、坊州、寧州、鞏昌、慶陽、慶州
*   **河源區域：** 崑崙山、悶磨黎山、星宿海、瑪曲、積石、葱嶺、于闐
*   **其他標記：** 十四（網格編號）、去寧河九十里、去甘州五百里等里程標註

<header>黃河圖四</header>

# 賈譲治河三策

->centered text<-
徙民防之，仁也。起戰國，齊、與、趙、魏以河為境。齊地卑下，作隄去河二十五里。
<-

非其正，水尚有所遊，及時而去，則填淤肥美，民耕田之。此皆前世所排室宅、行上業、而居之湛湯，固其宜也。當水衝者，放令北入海，此功一立，河定、民安，千載無患，謂之上策。

**中策：**若通多穿渠，旱則開民<term>埭</term>，水則截其<term>埭</term>，北入海者，功一立，河定、民安，千載無患，謂之中策也。之常也。然而木泥完故堤，不增厚倍、薄、勢費、無已，卒後亦必潰，此最下策也。

---

# 歐陽修曰

河若泥完故堤，不增厚倍、薄、勢費、無已，卒後亦必潰，此最下策也。治河之性、理、故、流、已、棄、之、道。流、自、古、難、復。行、新、策、乃、決、上、流、之、低、廢。此、勢之、常、也。然、則、木、泥、完、故、堤、不、增、厚、倍、薄、勢、費、無、已、卒、後、亦、必、潰、此、下、策、也。非不能、必、則、其、後、不、久、終、必、決、於、上、流、者、由、故、道、淤、而、水、不、能、行、故、也。智者、之、於、事、有、所不能、必、則、其、防、利、害、之、一、輕、重、必、擇、於、利、多、而、害、少、者、而、為、之、猶、愈、於、多、害、而、利、少。

# 歐陽修曰

至正之防、謂之河、一、河、有、三、其、一、為、諸、州、之、損、害、二、為、河、之、別、流、三、曰、生、地、曰、故、道、曰、河陽、之、因、而、深、之。河、生、地、之、直、者、也。因、直、而、鑿、之、故、道、有、高、有、卑。高、者、平、之、以、趨、卑。高、者、則高、不、壅、卑、水、而、滯、慮、夫、壅、生、有、潰、決、之、憂。若、河、身、之、直、者、則、可、引、而、之。狹、者、以、殺、其、怒、治、一、岸、為、岸、岸、則、有、物、以、詰、禦、之、計、禦、之、名、有、五。曰、有、高、有、卑、高、者、平、之、以隄、有、石、船、囚、其、怒。治、一、岸、為、岸。有、有、紉、築、係、故、者、河、身、之、直、者、則、引、而、趨、之。狹、者、則、難、以、受、水。若之、法。有、用、船、石、月、鐵、也。也有、有、岸、有、紉、築、係、故、者、河、身、之、直、者、則、引、而、趨、之。狹、者、則、難、以、受、水。若新河入故道之源也。曰為水所豁水退則下於水塞河則一也有有紉有罄口有水者有之龍口缺口也。

---

<margin>
劉真名言此地故
</margin>

## 宋濂曰

夫以數千里湍悍難治之河，而欲使一准以疏其怒勢，萬萬無此理也。分其半水使之分為一，則全其力。河治之要，執於此。

<margin>
先朝宋司空禮陳平江瑄之經理亦惟導汶建閘不復引河且於北岸築堤捲埽歲費億計防
</margin>
<margin>
河北徙如防寇盗然百餘年來縱遇早澇亦不越盤剥寄頃及抵京稍遲爾未始有壅塞
</margin>
<margin>
不通之患也惟汶泉之流過早則微匯水諸湖以淤而使引河之議或亦應此然
</margin>
<margin>
國計所繫當圖萬全魚已吾率引沁之為愈爾益勞費正藝而下以資河水之入而後策廣
</margin>
<margin>
旱則約之俾東入運易於節制之為萬全也若徐岱二洪而下則必資河水之入而後策廣
</margin>
<margin>
惟當時疏濬慎防禦相高下順逆之宜酌緩急輕重之勢因其所向而利導之爾
</margin>

## 劉天和曰

河之水至則衝決，退則於填而廢壞，閘座衝廣，河身阻隔，泉源害豈小耶。前此張秋之決，廟道口之於新河之後，今兹數百里之淤，可鑒已。議者有引狼兵以除內冠之喻。

<vocab>賈譲, 治河三策, 歐陽修, 宋濂, 劉天和, 黃河, 隄, 埭, 紉築, 龍口, 宋禮, 陳瑄, 狼兵</vocab>

<header>海運三</header>
<page-num>第九十八</page-num>

朱清張瑄者海上亡命也又為盜咄若風與倪錫等商人共苦之至元二十一年<gloss>伯顏建議海運乃招二人授以金符</gloss>千戶押運糧三萬五十石仍立海運道萬戶府三以清瑄與羅璧為萬戶帖千戶百戶所領虎符金牌乘牌船大者道不通由石小者三百石劉家港出海泛洋經界河黃連沙南沙三州黑水洋轉而北由通州至直沽徑延直至直沽山五月行到九皐島劉家島放洋又從界河兩月餘始至直沽轉糧由重慶平山等處至直沽轉輸成洋四月直沽交卸五月還復離遼至八月回一歲兩運是時船小人恐轉船不便元二十七年萬戶請長運至福州不自揚子江開洋落潞湖東北行至直沽通便利是歲加朱清為江浙省參政張瑄為新江海運司都運如是者三十一年除大德七年招兩浙上戶自造船與腳價十兩五錢分撥歲加朱清為江浙省參政張瑄為新江海運司都運如是者三十一年除大德七年招兩浙京師稱便逾南蕃貢亦通蓋自上每至直沽内楊村馬頭凡三萬三千三百五十八千九百二十餘石是皆清瑄張瑄港出福州經營崇明以北古未有之利也

朝洪武三十年循做其制歲運七十萬石給遼東至永樂間通會河城始不復講議者恒有意外之慮以為人生一日食不下咽則死亡立至會通河固南北之咽喉也訪求故道擇才而任之且重其權抑亦可為北事之防即便使有如清與瑄者亦足以資其馳備而誘以目效益博和未王憲使猷猷探源河之說因其甚成之功特以畫一之法俾表裏無遺焉恭酌利害以甦漕卒之困而求無疆之利要君子豈無是心失

<column-break/>

海運一福道福建布政司水波門船廠要水手船護送其神仙營水屋山島有戰閘送至河口一關防東南國中作潮平至望琪糖娘廟前泊一日至長樂港口一日至長遠巡檢司一州左等衙門要水手一送一日至五虎廟統總福一日至五虎門開洋望東北行正東便是福清縣堡場一日至五虎廟統總福一日至此高山巡檢司西洋山港口一日至福寧縣秦前海所山防霧曉夜觀體船日至金鄉衛要水手送一日松門衛一日至温州府戶所船送南東比風巡檢司官港口一日至金鄉衛要水手送一日松門衛一日至温州府陽縣巡檢司泊至鳳凰到州船支待防禦要風南巡檢司海口一日至海寧縣大龍山候潮行至松門衛見東北洋山泊莊品温州望北行千戶所東門泊所東龍觀夜觀利洋洋雞籠山候潮行至盤石衛見東北洋山龍門衛山至台州府海風門執行至青門山燒島石礁開洋至海大衛放頭獅子山水至健跳巡檢司過至松門衛先于所南開泊要風南開洋望前余千戶所雙磐山候潮過至大松千戶所石家山後水浅半船送又雲公堡漢津海衛南門一羅松江府上海縣海內悉山一座便是延真島海情海口浅灘避之

海風一一日見江浙望北行至遮洋山候潮行至白茆港候潮過至小七山大倉寶塔望東北行兩日夜見黑水洋南劉家港出海碯子江候洋水浅望東北行晚泊漁船見崇明過至大山水三三腳浪潮開之江內朝西行半日到白茆港港南平帶蓮槍橋遇檟腳沙尖轉崇明江北有三腳浪潮開之江內朝西行半日到白茆港港南平帶蓮槍橋遇檟腳沙尖轉崇明江内東有三腳浪潮開之江內朝西行半日到白茆港港南平帶蓮槍橋遇檟腳沙尖轉崇明江以北潮頭潮開之江内朝西南二西見塔山見南岸長灘是沙可避西北東外有暗礁一帶連至崇明時不見山見黑水或多或延為高望可見非羅珊海中中島名也山上有架架山卽後回望北帶西一字行一日夜見北岸山山若過黒洋水黑如墨或云正北行可以為便或復回望北東北二行比一山有馬鞍山山山島山北有旱門受灘避皆可泊若望山北行不可以繫北向礙橫百餘里浪浪如雪卽開使或便見或遠望見島山西北比西行十多步必是直了卽便後田望北東行可入西北成山正西行有馬鞍山嗚咽門向便是成山山如在比洋官錄水不深内望見到鳳島山可泊劉家山正西北行到界島東北行過東海面水西行到成山正西行有黑礁三四畝海潮之西有大夫人與天與洋官不深内望見劉鳳島山可泊劉家山正西北行到界島東北行過成山西行過北成山西南行有新灘海口洋到洋津西北行過東海面水西行到成山正西行有黑礁三四畝海潮之西有大夫人與天與洋官不深内望見劉鳳島山可泊劉家山正西北行到界島東北行過成山西行過北成山西南行有新灘海口洋到洋津到洋津到洋津到洋津
朝南行好風一日夜見綠水又一日夜見黑水又一日夜見白水望南見山西二一字行一日黑無正
南行成五十五尺深水下有亂泥二尺深黑水是長灘西北行西北行南望山背見沙沙島沙山背正
沙洪如不着洪望東南南行日看黃綠二色浪花如茶末夜看浪透如火星多卽是茶山若船坐茶山往西南一字半朝北見

崇明水南見青浦墩岸 劉家港如在黑水洋正南挑西多是高了前有陰沙半洋沙攔頭沙聞是曉角嘴便轉正南
座便望正西一字訊 往日沙洪水便是茶山如不見東南西北多必是低了見臨平則西
座東有望着水色風一向行收三日夜便知風向如何不見諸界山又不見茶山見黑水多便望正南行必見石龍山孫離山復
回望西南行見茶山收一洪至缺山帶東三字望南行經成山入南洋望正南行三日夜經桃花潭水望東行見白水帶西
達河口開洋順風一日夜到天山
二字勸截點竿尋長雜如一更八尺又漸減至一丈五尺望西行戳揚子江洪如不見望下使必見茶山鯽稍南面坐茶山望
西北行望見崇明洲如風順一朝至劉家港內

<header>漕運圖 每方一百里</header>
<page-num>第二十一</page-num>

<image-desc>
這是一幅中國古代木版印刷的「漕運圖」，採用「計里畫方」的測繪方式，方格註明「每方一百里」。地圖描繪了京杭大運河與黃河、淮河交匯處的關鍵河段，特別是江蘇淮安至徐州一帶的漕運路線。

地圖方位標記如下：
- **左側邊緣**：標註「東」，對應鹽城、安東等沿海地區。
- **右側邊緣**：標註「南」，對應揚州府、鎮江府等靠近長江的地帶。
- **下方邊緣**：標註「西」，對應徐州府及黃河上游方向。

地圖詳盡展示了城鎮（以城牆符號表示）、山脈（傳統山水畫法）、湖泊（如駱馬湖、微山湖系）以及河流渠道。地圖上密布標籤，記錄了各個閘、壩、洪（灘險）以及行政建制。
</image-desc>

<margin>東</margin>
<margin>南</margin>
<margin>西</margin>

<header>漕運圖二 每方一百里</header>

<image-desc>
這是一幅木刻印刷的中國古代地理地圖，屬於《漕運圖》系列。地圖上覆蓋有計里畫方的方格，左側注記「每方一百里」。圖中描繪了京杭大運河（會通河）的山東段及其周邊水系。主要河流包括黃河、泗水、汶河、白馬河等。地圖上詳細標註了運河沿線的關鍵水利設施，如閘（天井閘、南旺閘、開河閘、柳林閘、十里閘等）、壩以及淺灘。地圖還標示了沿線的重要城鎮，如濟寧州、東阿、陽穀、壽張、鄆城、恩縣、武城等。地圖的方位標註在左右兩側，分別為「北」與「南」，顯示運河由北向南的走勢（在此圖中為自左向右）。
</image-desc>

<header>漕運圖三 每方百里</header>

<image-desc>
A historical Chinese map depicting the northern section of the Grand Canal and surrounding river systems. The map is drawn over a rectangular grid. 

- **Top Center:** A large shaded area labeled "東" (East) represents the sea (Bohai Sea).
- **Left Side:** Mountainous terrain is depicted with traditional brushwork, with labels for "北" (North) and several northern towns.
- **Waterways:** Multiple rivers and canals are shown flowing toward the sea and southwards (to the right). 
- **Annotations:** Numerous labels designate administrative seats (towns/cities) and specific nautical notes such as "淺" (shallows/sandbars) with associated numbers.
- **Border:** The page number is located in the bottom left corner within the frame.
</image-desc>

### 歲運

- **洪武三十年**海運糧七十萬石于遼東
- **永樂六年**海運糧六十六萬二千四百二十石于北京
- 十二年於運海運上二百萬四千八百二十一石于通州衛河儲運撥過古萬二千七百七十六石于北京
- **宣德八年**備運糧五百餘萬石通倉收二分京倉收一分
- 十六年會通河漕運漸窄撥准淮安等處歲建倉糧四百四十万六千五百三十石于北京
- **正統二年**備運糧四百五十五萬九千六百九十八石
- **景泰二年**備運糧四百二十三萬五千石
- 七年備運糧三百九十一万三千五百九石二斗一升 兌米二百八十二万三千三百四十八石 支運米一百九萬一百六十石
- **天順四年**備運糧四百三十五萬九千八百七十五石 兌米三百一万六千二百三十石 支運米一百三十四万三千六百二十石
- **成化八年**備運糧四百萬石
- **弘治二年**備運糧三百九十四萬五千四百石 兌運粮三百三十萬石 支運倉粮六十四万五千四百石
- **正德六年**備運糧四百四十万零九石 運糧三百二万石 兌米三十二萬三千五百石 支運倉粮八十萬石
- **嘉靖元年**備運糧四百四萬五千六百石 運糧三百一十二萬石 兌米八十二萬四千四百石 支運倉粮五萬六千石

<vocab>漕運, 衛, 所, 把總, 歲運, 額粮, 支米, 兌米, 支運, 洪武, 永樂, 宣德, 正統, 景泰, 天順, 成化, 弘治, 正德, 嘉靖</vocab>

<page-type>map</page-type>
<header>朝鮮圖 每方一百里</header>
<meta>catchword/folio: 第二百五</meta>

<image-desc>
A historical woodblock print map of the Korean Peninsula (Joseon dynasty), oriented with North at the top. The map is overlaid with a coordinate grid where each square represents 100 li (每方一百里), as indicated by the title box in the upper right.

To the northwest, across the Yalu River (鴨綠江), regions of the Liaodong Peninsula/Manchuria are labeled with names such as 瀋陽 (Shenyang), 遼陽 (Liaoyang), 盛京 (Shengjing), and 寬奠 (Kuandian). Mount Paektu is marked as 白山 at the northern tip.

The peninsula itself is densely labeled with administrative districts, mountains, and rivers. The eight provinces (Do) are prominently identified:
- 平安道 (Pyeongan-do)
- 咸鏡道 (Hamgyeong-do)
- 黃海道 (Hwanghae-do)
- 京畿道 (Gyeonggi-do)
- 江原道 (Gangwon-do)
- 忠清道 (Chungcheong-do)
- 慶尚道 (Gyeongsang-do)
- 全羅道 (Jeolla-do)

Major cities like 義州 (Uiju), 平壤 (Pyongyang), and various regional capitals are noted. The surrounding seas are depicted with stylized wave patterns and populated with numerous offshore islands, including 濟州 (Jeju) to the south and 鬱陵島 (Ulleungdo) to the east. The bottom left margin contains the folio marker "第二百五".
</image-desc>

# 東南海夷圖

界内每方四百里界外海中風不常唯以里程載

<image-desc>
A historical Chinese woodblock-printed map titled "Map of the Barbarians in the Southeast Sea" (東南海夷圖). The map illustrates the coastal regions of Southern and Eastern China and the surrounding maritime world of Southeast and East Asia.

The continental area in the upper left, representing the Chinese Ming Empire, is overlaid with a square grid. A vertical note on the left explains the scale: "界内每方四百里界外海中風不常唯以里程載" (Inside the border, each square is 400 li; outside the border in the sea, winds are not constant, so only distances are recorded). The sea areas are depicted with stylized wave patterns.

Numerous place names, often enclosed in small oval borders, are scattered across the map:
- **Coastal and Inland China:** Includes 南京 (Nanjing), 杭州 (Hangzhou), 寧波 (Ningbo), 溫州 (Wenzhou), 福州 (Fuzhou), 泉州 (Quanzhou), 漳州 (Zhangzhou), 潮州 (Chaozhou), 廣州 (Guangzhou), and 瓊州 (Qiongzhou/Hainan).
- **Southeast Asia (Left and Center):** Includes 安南 (Annam/Vietnam), 占城 (Champa), 眞臘 (Chenla/Cambodia), 暹羅 (Siam/Thailand), 滿剌加 (Malacca), 蘇門答剌 (Sumatra), 舊港 (Palembang), 爪哇 (Java), and 浡泥 (Brunei/Borneo).
- **East Asia (Right):** Includes 琉球 (Ryukyu), 日本 (Japan), and 高麗 (Goryeo/Korea).
- **Islands:** Various smaller islands such as 呂宋 (Luzon), 蘇祿 (Sulu), and 崑崙山 (Pulo Condore) are labeled.

The folio number "第一百零七" (107) is printed in the bottom right corner.
</image-desc>

<vocab>東南海夷圖, 安南, 占城, 眞臘, 暹羅, 滿剌加, 蘇門答剌, 爪哇, 浡泥, 蘇祿, 呂宋, 琉球, 日本, 高麗, 里程</vocab>

<image-desc>
A traditional Chinese nautical chart featuring a square grid overlay and stylized wave patterns representing the ocean. The map depicts coastal regions and islands with landmasses located at the top and on the right side of the page. Place names are enclosed in small ovals or rectangular frames. This map belongs to the "Mao Kun map" (also known as the Zheng He Navigation Map) included in the military treatise "Wubei Zhi" (Treatise on Armament Technology), showing parts of the Arabian Peninsula and the East African coast. Key locations such as Hormuz (忽魯謨斯), Medina (默德那), and Mecca (天堂) are identifiable.
</image-desc>

# 安南圖二

安南本古南交地秦象郡即漢交趾九真日南三郡治贏婁吳改九德武平新昌宋改宋平徙龍編梁安南鎮南都護五代之亂推丁部領為帥宋封其子璿交趾郡王後黎桓李公蘊陳日煚相繼篡立又五世為黎季犛所篡永樂四年遣兵平之建交趾布政司領府十七州五屬州四十一縣一百五十七宣德二年黎利復叛遣兵討之利懼奉表乞立陳氏後

朝廷許之因罷郡縣已而利篡陳自立嘉靖六年其孫登庸乘黎之亂弒黎慮自立僭國號曰大越大越改元明德子方瀛嗣改元大正且侵內地十八年遣兵討之登庸表降請罪願歸侵地於是朝廷赦登庸罪署子方瀛安南都統使遂罷兵然其專擅自若也

->安南等處承宣布政使司 按察司 都指揮使司 治交州府宣德三年始削縣<-

### 廣南承政司 <gloss>卽廣南承政司三府領南番韶江廣</gloss>

入交三道一由廣西一由廣東一由雲南 <gloss>由廣東則用水軍伏波以來皆行之廣西道宋行之雲南道</gloss>
元及我
朝始開廣西道亦分為三從憑祥州入者 <gloss>由州南南關隘</gloss> 一日至交之文淵州 <gloss>坡壘驛</gloss> 後經脫朗州北一日
至諒山衙又一日至溫州之北 <gloss>險徑半日</gloss> 至鬼門關又一日經溫州之南 <gloss>新莊村</gloss> 經二下江一日至
保祿縣半日渡昌江又一日至安越縣南 <gloss>市橋江下流北岸</gloss> 一道由思明府入過摩天嶺一日至鬼
陵州過辨強隘一日至祿平州 <gloss>州西有路一日半</gloss> 至諒山府若從東南行過車里江 <gloss>此江永樂中黎</gloss>
季斄蹙之以拒王師後領州知其艱險乃决一河以濟 <gloss>一日至安博州又一日半過耗軍峒山險</gloss>
惡又一日至鳳眼縣又分二道一道一日至保祿縣亦渡昌江至安博府亦一日至安越縣之
南市橋江北岸各與前道會其自龍州入者一日至平而隘又一日至七源州 <gloss>二百</gloss> 至文蘭平而社
又分為二道一道從文蘭州 <gloss>一曰至德江</gloss> 一由山徑出鬼門關下 <gloss>十四日</gloss> 至涼江府上源經右布南
沿江南岸而下一日至世蘭安縣平地至安勇縣又一日亦至安越縣之中市橋江北岸一道從平而
社西一日半經武崖州山徑二日至司農縣平地又一日半亦進至安越縣之北市橋江上流北岸
市橋江在安越縣境中昌江之南諸路總會之處處處皆可駐師一日至至交州之府又至東潮海州等
縣渡富良江以入交州雲南亦有二道其一道由蒙自縣經蓮花灘一日至交州之石隴關又一日至清
洮江源右岸四日至水尾州 <gloss>即富良江上游</gloss> 文盤州又五日至鎮安縣又五日 <gloss>曰至者野縣又三日至</gloss>
縣又三日至臨洮府洮江水便且流其北一為宜光江江至宜光府又五日至五日 <gloss>曰至也三縣渡富良</gloss>
東一道自河陽白隘循洮江左岸十日至興化一日至白鶴神廟為洮江三岐江又四日至 <gloss>至者野江</gloss>
其一府亦至白鶴州以入後由洮江二日至宣光府又五日至三岐江又一日至宣光府大道也若端
海府白蔗州馬雷山竣舟西北順利一日可抵交之海東府沿海岸以行則馬雷山一日至察州
安州白龍尾 <gloss>白龍尾</gloss> 二日至玉山門又一日至陳萬寧州萬寧一日至廟山一日至安海縣巡
二日至海東海東府二日至至至經熱口又有石堤海口各有兵守入交州自白藤海口入則不掌
司南至安海縣陽海口又南至至至山熟口又南至多魚海口以兵守之自白藤海口入則經安
東潮二縣至海陽府後經至雲山過黃徑弄平灘等江其自安陽海口入則經安陽縣至荊門府
至黃徑等江由南策上洪之北境以入其自多魚海口而入則由安老古齋又取宜興安老至快
河縣經南策上洪之南境以入其自多魚海口而入則由安老古齋又取宜興安老至快州經
鹹子關以入多漁南為太平海口其路由太平新興二府亦經快州府去海頗遠各有支港穿連
道之大要也交州之東有海陽荆門南策以上洪順安快府等府去海頗遠各有支港穿連
數百里大艦不能入故交人多平底淺舟以便入港云

<lang>zh</lang>
<page-type>map</page-type>
<header>西域圖 每方百里</header>
<meta>北</meta>
<meta>第一百十二</meta>

<image-desc>
This is a historical woodblock-printed map of the "Western Regions" (西域圖), oriented with North at the top (indicated by the character "北" in a small box in the upper left). The map is superimposed with a grid, and a label in the top right box specifies "每方百里" (each square represents 100 li). It depicts a vast geographic area including modern-day Central Asia, India, and Southeast Asia. 

Mountains are represented by clusters of stylized peaks, and rivers by winding lines. The map is densely populated with names of ancient kingdoms, cities, and landmarks, many of which correspond to accounts found in historical Chinese Buddhist travelogues (such as those by Xuanzang). 

The right side of the map shows southwestern Chinese border regions and neighboring countries like Burma (緬甸), Siam (暹羅), and Annam (安南). The central and left portions focus on "Tianzhu" (天竺), the historical Chinese name for India, divided into five regions (North, South, East, West, and Central). Small text annotations provide additional geographical or historical context for various locations. The page number "第一百十二" (112) is located in the bottom left corner.
</image-desc>

# 朔漠圖

自錦州西奚以東故皆東夷地其西則北狄也東夷之國不一其後或并于遼金遼金相滅種類殆盡今則雜居處之而建州海西各夷亦不相統其北所謂韃靼室韋與狗國之屬道理邈絕有無不可知矣北狄自匈奴突厥以後劉淵父子始入中國而莫盛於胡元遂至混一四海華夷無外固開闢以來未有之變亦南北氣運之迴薄也夫汛掃備難

二祖之功備矣畢竟相尋理勢當然百年生聚加以馴致履霜之漸可無備哉可無備哉

---

<lang>zh-Hant</lang>
<page-type>diagram</page-type>
<page-num>第百五十五</page-num>
<header>四夷圖二</header>

# 四夷圖二

## 東夷
**朝鮮國** <gloss>王姓李，武德中，高麗王高建武遣使入貢。武德七年封為高麗王。後其臣泉蓋蘇文弑其主，立建武弟之子藏。高宗顯慶五年遣蘇定方、契苾何力等討之。總章元年，李勣拔平壤城，虜藏。分其地為九都督府。元世祖封王禃。我朝洪武初遣使入貢，賜印。後王旦上表更名，賜號朝鮮，居漢城。</gloss>

**日本** <gloss>文曰倭，在海中，歷代皆通。我朝洪武三年，其王懷良遣使入貢。五年封其子良懷為國王。永樂初封源道義為日本國王。賜金印冠服。給勘合。十年一貢。船二隻。人二百人。貢物：硫黃、蘇木、珊瑚、水銀、腰刀、扇。其人剽悍，好鬥。嘉靖以來，中朝法禁不嚴，奸民導之入寇。後王義元復由寧波入貢。我朝仍嚴禁之。其人剽悍，性好賊殺。我中朝人呼為倭寇。</gloss>

**大琉球** <gloss>王姓閔氏。洪武中領國分一為三。遣使入貢。自中山、山北、山南王。其後後為中山所併。封王。不絶。永樂十一年，長史梁守、副使楊景入貢。自宣德以後，貢使仍不絕。</gloss>

**小琉球** <gloss>與大琉球相近。永樂中曾入貢。自後未至。</gloss>

<column-break/>

## 東南夷
**安南國** <gloss>古交趾，秦置象郡。漢唐以來屬中原。後其臣黎季犛簒其主。長子自立。成祖永樂中封黎氏。既而季犛奪政。我朝發兵討之。永樂六年平定。設郡縣。後至宣德元年，復封黎利為國王。不絕。其地東西南至海。北至廣西思恩府界。我朝分其境為三。一安南，二占城，三暹羅。其地與廣西接。國人狡詐好鬥。</gloss>

**占城** <gloss>古林邑國，隋南武郡。洪武二年，王阿答阿者遣使入貢。賜印。永樂初，其國王孫復來貢。亦其先世自朝貢。不絕。</gloss>

**爪哇** <gloss>在古闍婆國。元遣兵萬人入其境，既而退歸。洪武初其王僧伽剌、八達、剌那、哈剌、王入貢。至永樂初，其王蘇甘剌與其主爭國，被誅。後王入貢。其地多水，俗好鬥。居室如舟。其土沃，五穀皆宜。其俗淳。其俗事佛，好鬥。有三佛齊。</gloss>

**三佛齊** <gloss>在古三佛齊國南。永樂初，王陳祖義遣使。既而祖義反，賜其副使施進卿。元末兵亂，後其國王三佛齊與其子爭國，我朝命其大臣領之。其地多金。俗事佛。朝貢不絕。</gloss>

**暹羅** <gloss>古暹國。洪武初其王參烈波羅遣使入貢。賜印。永樂三年封其子。不絕。其國在山。俗事佛。其俗好鬥。其地多產木。其地東南至海。西北接雲南。國人多入寇。萬曆以後不絕。三月。未其夏。</gloss>

**真臘** <gloss>在古林邑西南。洪武初其王參烈。嘉靖以後不絶。萬曆中其王與其子爭國，我朝分其地。封其子為王。其俗好鬥。其地多金。朝貢不絶。</gloss>

<column-break/>

## 南夷
**浡泥** <gloss>在西南海。洪武初其王馬合謨沙遣使入貢。永樂六年其王麻那惹加那來朝。卒于京。其子自立。其俗好鬥。其地多金。不絕。</gloss>

**蘇祿** <gloss>其國與浡泥接。永樂十五年其東王巴都葛叭、答剌西王麻那、剌王入貢。不絕。其俗好鬥。其地多金。朝貢不絕。</gloss>

**古里** <gloss>其國與滿剌加國王未領。永樂五年遣使入貢。賜印。永樂三年其王與其子爭國。其俗好鬥。其地多金。朝貢不絕。</gloss>

**滿剌加** <gloss>其國與蘇門答剌接。永樂初其王拜里迷蘇剌入貢。賜印。宣德初其子入貢。其俗好鬥。其地多金。朝貢不絕。</gloss>

**蘇門答剌** <gloss>其國與蘇祿接。永樂初其王蘇干剌入貢。後永樂十一年其子入貢。其俗好鬥。其地多金。朝貢不絕。</gloss>

**錫蘭山** <gloss>其國與柯枝接。永樂七年遣使入貢。後王入貢。其俗事佛。其地多金。朝貢不絕。</gloss>

**柯枝** <gloss>其國與蘇祿國王未領。永樂十五年遣使入貢。賜印。其俗事佛。其地多金。朝貢不絕。</gloss>

<column-break/>

<margin>地在開原北至金之上京，北極度星，始高通明初開版圖。建女直衛奴兒干都司衛所，始督指揮等，其始亦非自宋建始。至此所居者亦非其初矣。後皆不可考其美。</margin>

### 北夷

<margin>韃靼 略云此元子孫其地在開原迤北即元之舊都也。永樂二十年，成祖親征。至其境之廣，山川道里，不容必載。水則有斡難、怯律、延、忽、蘭、忽、剌河。
其人名色非一，王爵元臣，不可胜紀。其臣子子孫散居境外。今之通款者有阿魯台，及其下王公大臣，其字曰蒙古，其人曰韃靼。
瓦剌 元之婿也。永樂六年，太宗遣使招諭之，封其長馬哈木為順寧王，太平為賢義王，把禿孛羅為安樂王。其後脫懽始強，其子也先益暴橫。
至正統十四年，犯境俘。上。自是瓦剌亦不可一言盡。而所謂也先者，已為其下阿剌知院所殺，而脫脫不花、不刺、伯顔。
帖木兒、阿剌，其徒或自立王。或自王其下，皆互相吞并。其境內名色不下三千。雖言人，而地亦連亘。其地在甘、凉迤北及寧夏、延、隋、宣府、大同、山海、喜峰各関以外。
蓋自韃靼分而為二，至也先後更變多端，今其國号，関外所稱呼與其人名号亦難考實。今略錄其在邊界大略者。
日落掩明。始正以下三十八圖。其詳有嘉靖或萬歷三年、五年。
日落掩明期入貢。不過唯掩後多。其部落名号，関外亦未敢考竟。</margin>

韃靼
瓦剌

---

雜谷安撫司 <gloss>元丞相，自元相國永不詔其境。地在岷州。開泰、永興、金川等處。明初，其長必里。洪武六年，遣使入貢</gloss>

### 西北夷

亦力把里 <gloss>地在西湖西，即元之。亦力把里在耳州。西即用哈密。與家隣。洪武間遣使。永樂年，遣使謝罪，後自稱王。其後屢入貢。至今正德嘉靖間常通。其主哈密。其後治政，或治、或亂</gloss>
哈密 <gloss>地在沙洲西，其元王。肅王、忠順王，其治此地。世守之。而亦自立。其人好生亂。自嘉靖中，土魯番取其城。後亦通貢。其部落散居者</gloss>
火州 <gloss>地在哈密西。本西域之高昌。地名火州。元為火州。明初封其王</gloss>

---

哈三
哈密
沙州
沙州
撒馬兒罕 <gloss>元屬西域。元封忠義王。地在西北。其長王其地。明初通</gloss>
松潘 <gloss>地在陝西西北。元時設松潘、茂州</gloss>
